## TR-castability criterion

A nano is included when one of its cast `Actions` (`ActionType.ToUse` = 3) has an `EqualTo` requirement on **`Profession`(stat 60) == 7** or **`VisualProfession`(stat 368) == 7**. VisualProfession is how most older profession nanos are locked. Split: 87 via Profession(60), 235 via VisualProfession(368). Every match uses the EqualTo operator.
